Not tried in-game yet.
- **Climbing done once for every body** (the upgrade review's M4 with B3, the spine; planned with the user 2026-10-18, "lets get started on climbing done once for everybody"; plan `docs/plans/climbing-every-body.md`; branch `climbing-every-body` from `brain-danger`). **Before:** every insect climbed with the harvester worker's numbers, only scaled by its size (feet 3.6 and 2.85 units, the waist and gaster 0.64 / 2.4 / 5.2 behind the middle, lift 0.25 and push 0.17 on a wall, the major soldier's 0.4 / 0.27 set by hand). **Now:** (1) `tools/body_plan.py measure <model>|all` writes each body plan's `climb` part (front and hind feet from the walk's average stance, front and rear end, the tightest curve) and its `spine` (each segment: its joints and where it lies behind the middle) from the model; `check` says when a model changed since; the worker keeps its approved numbers (the tool reports how far its own measuring would put them). (2) The game reads them: `BodyPlans.Plan` holds the spine pieces and `Climb`; `EdgeOver` takes the feet from the insect's plan; `AntRenderer` takes the lift and push from it, or works them out from the body's size and length when a plan has none (`Climb.liftFor`, `intoWallFor`, calibrated so the worker comes out at exactly 0.25 and 0.17); `withClimbPose` is gone. (3) **The spine** (`entity/body/Spine`, body only): the path memory and "follow the leader" move out of `AbstractAntEntity`, for any number of segments: each segment lies the way the path lay over its own stretch behind, each joint bent by the difference with the segment in front, held within its own reach. `SpineTest`: the old waist code kept word for word replays climbs (up a wall, over a top, a brink, a turn, a stop half way over, 3 random walks, at two sizes) through the old and the new: equal to the last bit; and a made-up 12-ring body walked round a turn, up a wall and over its top lies along its path, flat at the end, no joint past its reach. (4) **Soft termite abdomens (the user's pick):** the worker's, both soldiers' and the king's abdomen are 6 chained segment bones, one per drawn plate (`abdomen`, `abdomen_2` to `abdomen_6`, `tools/termite_models.py`; the plan said 4, 6 matches the plates; my pick, to confirm), each a spine piece bending at most 15 degrees up or down and 6 to the side (my picks); the clips are unchanged (a clip swaying `abdomen` sways all of it); the small queen keeps her one piece. (5) **Proof:** `./gradlew build` (with `BodyPlansTest.everyBodyClimbsWithItsOwnNumbers` and `SpineTest`); the lab finds nothing new on the termites (their old 2 BAD each, an antenna tip and a front leg, are the same on the old models); new guard scenes `k_climb_minor`, `l_climb_major`, `m_climb_king` (up a pillar two blocks high, over its top and head first down; the king starts nearer and gets 130 ticks, as it walks slowly); every ant guard film picture for picture the same as before (a_walk, b_turn, c_climb, d_ledge, e_ceiling, h_crowd; and i_soldier, j_king, walking only, too); `f_termite` changed on purpose (the old abdomen jump of 0.21 at the pillar's foot gone); game tests `legs,body` 23 of 23 on 8 servers. **Found and fixed by the new scenes** (`docs/BUGS.md` 52): the minor soldier flipped at the foot of a wall (its feet reach less far than its box), the major's jaws went into the stone (its head reaches far past its feet); now a body put on the wall early is drawn lying on the floor till its feet get there, and the tipping starts when the front feet or the head reach the wall. **Known, approved as known bugs** (`docs/BUGS.md` 53, 54): coming head first down onto the floor termites stop and turn round on the spot, then dash (the walking code, old); going head first over a top the rear points up into the air (the spine follows the thorax's earlier tilt, not the ground; the ant does it too; the fix changes the approved ant, so it waits for the user; fixed the same day, next bullet). Not tried in-game yet.
- **The rear follows the ground under the hind feet** (2026-10-18, the user's OK: "make the rear follow the ground under the hind feet for every body, the ant too"; branch `climbing-every-body`; fixes `docs/BUGS.md` 54). **Before:** the spine remembered the thorax's tilt along the path, and the thorax tips over an edge from when its front feet get there, so going head first over a top the rear (the ant's gaster, a termite's abdomen) pointed up into the air, up to 45 degrees. **Now:** `Spine` remembers the ground under the hind feet (the front's pitch less the thorax's lean across an edge or a corner, `EdgeOver.Pose.fromTilt` naming the surface the hind feet stand on, `AbstractAntEntity.edgeFromTilt`); a piece's part in front of the hind feet lies along the thorax, its part behind them on the ground walked there; each piece is judged by its own body (`from`, the ant's gaster from 2.4 units); the old waist's little trick for a head-down edge is gone. `SpineTest`: the ant's waist still turns sideways bit for bit as before; head first over a top, a 6-segment abdomen and the ant: every joint behind the hind feet bends toward the top as far as it reaches, the rear never higher than the old rule put it (more than 20 degrees lower at worst), straight down the wall after. Guard films: `c_climb`, `d_ledge`, `e_ceiling` (a slightly bigger bend going onto the ceiling), `f_termite`, `k_climb_minor`, `l_climb_major` (the rear stays on the floor going up), `m_climb_king` approved anew; new, approved as known: the ant's gaster grazes the edge's corner up to 0.03 blocks crossing it (`c_climb`, `d_ledge`), and the termite's abdomen unrolls in 2 ticks at the pillar's foot because of the navigator's dash (53). The film log (`times.txt`) now lists every spine piece's bend. Before and after pictures in `references/rear_follows_ground/`. Game tests `legs,body` 23 of 23 on 8 servers. Not tried in-game yet.
- **Fix: off a wall, straight out before turning** (2026-10-18, branch `climbing-every-body`, the user's report: "they go down a block and slide to the left or right ... before actually turning", "their abdomens are still raised when they turn"; `docs/BUGS.md` 56). `AntNavigator.offWall` has a second part: once the body has tipped back onto the floor it walks straight out from the wall by its box's width (worker 0.3, major soldier 0.4 blocks, the most a cell allows) and only then hands over to the next step, which turns at once. Films before and after on a ridge one block wide: the worker swung 110 degrees in 2 ticks while tipped 45 degrees with the waist bent 60 and slid sideways; now it walks straight 4 ticks, flat, then turns. New guard scenes `n_down_turn` (worker) and `o_down_turn_major`; `c_climb`, `f_termite`, `k_climb_minor`, `m_climb_king` approved again (only the foot of each climb down changed). Found on the way: a tilt wobble going over the side edge of a ridge (57, open).
- **Merge: `climbing-every-body`'s tree shape changes into `master`** (2026-10-19, the user's OK, no full run: tree shapes only, the user's call): the camel thorn limbs (a full log at the trunk, thinner every 2 blocks) and the tapering branch blocks (`TaperingBranchBlock`, 67 percent at the start). Four clashes with step 5 joined by hand: `TreeDraw.growing` keeps both step 5's `taperOver` (broken limbs) and the thick growing tip (`tipMin`); `tree_art.py` keeps the charred wood and makes the bleached branch a tapering one; two imports in `TreePlacer`; the CLAUDE.md status line. The branch blocks keep their names, so they are still in `termite_wood`. `./gradlew build` passes; `trees,termitefood,away` 54 of 54 on 4 servers.
- **Fix: dead gallery branches grown on before they were filled in** (2026-10-19, `docs/BUGS.md` 59; the flaky `aTermiteColonyAwayForagesFromTheNightsHolesAndGrowsItsGalleriesOnReturn`, failing about half its runs alone). Just back from being away, the builders' time could grow a new piece from the tip of a branch that had led to no food for 11 days, before the catch-up filled it in; then that branch could never be filled. Now a gallery piece due to be filled in is never explored further at random (as with a way already backfilled; real Macrotermes do not dig on along a gallery that led nowhere, they pack it with soil); food can still draw it on. `Galleries.isDue`. The test passes 6 of 6 alone; `termitefood,away` 37 of 38 on 6 servers (6.5 minutes; the one failure the old fallen tree up a slope flake, which passed 3 of 3 alone). Nothing new to see in-game.
- **Fix: at the foot of a wall, no more stop, spin and dash; over a ridge's side edge, no more wobble** (2026-10-18, branch `body-wall-fixes` from `climbing-every-body`; `docs/BUGS.md` 53 and 57). Legs only (`AntNavigator`, the let-go in `AbstractAntEntity`); the body code untouched. (53) On a wall the yaw now follows the head (wall yaw minus heading), the walk out at the foot is an exact motion at the climbing pace, and an insect letting go onto a floor keeps its pace and stands on it at once: the king and the minor soldier no longer turn 180 degrees on the spot and creep then dash at the foot of a climb down. (57) Only a motion mostly along the wall turns the head on it, and a hair's correction never does: the worker going head first over a ridge's side edge tips over smoothly (0 to 90 in 11 ticks). Proved with the walkers' logs and a server log before and after; guard films: `c_climb`, `d_ledge`, `f_termite`, `k_climb_minor`, `m_climb_king`, `n_down_turn` (known bug cleared), `o_down_turn_major` approved again, the rest unchanged (`i_soldier` changed once in a full run and not when refilmed: timing noise); `legs` 21 of 21 on 8 servers; build passes. Not tried in-game yet.
